    def test_c56_changed_paths_stay_on_the_declared_edit_surface():
        status = [s.split("\t")[-1] for s in changed_name_status()]
        allowed_exact = {
            "scripts/python/feedback-utils.py",
            "scripts/python/probe-utils.py",
            "shared/guidelines/dogfooding.md",
            "shared/workflow/self-improvement-workflow.md",
            "skills/internal/code-review/SKILL.md",
            "skills/internal/code-review/scripts/git-delta-review.sh",
            "skills/internal/manage-feedback/SKILL.md",
            "skills/internal/manage-feedback/references/path-a-digest.md",
            "skills/internal/self-improvement/SKILL.md",
            "templates/commands/feedback.md",
            "templates/commands/improve.md",
            "templates/commands/review.md",
            # ID register is updated by review's REC-registration duty (071/063
            # obligations) — a legitimate project record, not a hand edit of
            # framework sources. Narrow exact entry; do NOT widen the runtime-store prefixes.
            ".specify/register.md",
        }
        allowed_prefixes = (
            "docs/reference/commands/",   # command reference docs (C-56)
            ".specify/specs/072-client-self-improvement/",   # this feature's spec artifacts
            ".specify/features",    # feature registry rows (072 line + detail)
            ".specify/exec-quant/records/",   # dogfooding loop records (new entries only)
            ".specify/session/",                # dogfooding loop records (new entries only)
            ".specify/feedback/",               # dogfooding loop records (new entries only)
            "tests/contract/", "tests/fixtures/",      # contract tests are the declared surface
            ".claude/commands/", ".github/prompts/",
            ".opencode/command/", ".qoder/commands/",  # per-tool copies (regenerated)
            ".specify/scripts/", ".specify/shared/",  # mirrors
            ".specify/skills/",  # all skill mirrors are produced by sync-mirrors (generated, never hand-edited)
        )
        offenders = []
        for rel in status:
            if rel in allowed_exact or rel.startswith(allowed_prefixes):
                continue
            if rel.startswith(".specify/scripts/"):
                continue
            offenders.append(rel)
>       assert not offenders, (

______________________ test_c7_mirrors_are_byte_identical ______________________
[gw132] linux -- Python 3.11.11 /usr/local/bin/python3

    def test_c7_mirrors_are_byte_identical():
        problems: list[str] = []
        for source_rel, mirror_rel in sorted(MIRROR_PAIRS.items()):
            source = REPO_ROOT / source_rel
            mirror = REPO_ROOT / mirror_rel
            assert source.is_file(), "owned source missing: %s" % source_rel
            if not mirror.is_file():
                if source_rel not in NEW_PAYLOAD_SOURCES:
                    problems.append("%s — mirror disappeared" % mirror_rel)
                continue
            if source.read_bytes() != mirror.read_bytes():
                problems.append("%s — mirror differs from %s" % (mirror_rel, source_rel))
>       assert not problems, (
            "C-7: mirrors are projections of the framework source, never hand-edited "
            "(absence is tolerated only for the four new payloads until T033):\n- "
            + "\n- ".join(problems)
        )
E       AssertionError: C-7: mirrors are projections of the framework source, never hand-edited (absence is tolerated only for the four new payloads until T033):

    def test_g17_mirrors_produced_by_engines_only():
        """G-17: the mirror pairs are unchanged, and mirrors are byte-identical to sources."""
        import importlib.util
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
            "_ff_sync_mirrors", ROOT / "scripts" / "python" / "sync-mirrors.py"
        )
        mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
        pairs = [(s, d) for s, d, _strict, _ex in mod.MIRROR_PAIRS]
        assert ("shared", ".specify/shared") in pairs, "G-17: the shared mirror pair is gone"
        assert ("agents", ".specify/agents/templates") in pairs, "G-17: the agents mirror pair is gone"
        assert ("templates", ".specify/templates") in pairs, "G-17: the templates mirror pair is gone"
        assert len(mod.MIRROR_PAIRS) == 6, f"G-17: MIRROR_PAIRS has {len(mod.MIRROR_PAIRS)} entries, expected 6 (skills split into external+internal)"
        # every artifact this feature mirrored is byte-identical to its source
        for src, mirror in (
            (DOC, DOC_MIRROR),
            (ROOT / "shared" / "definitions" / "subagent-definitions.md",
             ROOT / ".specify" / "shared" / "definitions" / "subagent-definitions.md"),
            (ROOT / "shared" / "guidelines" / "user-facing-comprehension.md",
             ROOT / ".specify" / "shared" / "guidelines" / "user-facing-comprehension.md"),
            (ROOT / "templates" / "instructions-template.md",
             ROOT / ".specify" / "templates" / "instructions-template.md"),
            (ROOT / "templates" / "constitution-template.md",
             ROOT / ".specify" / "templates" / "constitution-template.md"),
        ):

______________ test_c6_feedback_step_edit_is_additive_single_line ______________
[gw135] linux -- Python 3.11.11 /usr/local/bin/python3

    def test_c6_feedback_step_edit_is_additive_single_line():
        proc = _git("diff", "-U0", PRE_CHANGE_HEAD, "--", "shared/workflow/feedback-step.md")
        assert proc.returncode == 0, "git diff failed: %r" % proc.stderr
        removed: list[str] = []
        added: list[str] = []
        for line in proc.stdout.splitlines():
            if line.startswith("---") or line.startswith("+++"):
                continue
            if line.startswith("-"):
                removed.append(line)
            elif line.startswith("+") and line[1:].strip():
                added.append(line[1:])
        declared_removals = {
            text for texts in C6_LATER_REMOVALS.values() for text in texts
        }
        undeclared = [line for line in removed if line[1:] not in declared_removals]
>       assert not undeclared, (
            "C-6: the existing complexity criterion and red-line paragraphs must stay "
            "byte-identical — undeclared removed lines: %r (declared: %r)"
            % (undeclared, sorted(declared_removals))

    def test_c63_a_status_line_and_a_digest_over_one_store_carry_equal_element_counts(tmp_path):

        )

    def test_time_filter_all_semantics_unchanged_payload_full(
            tmp_path, capsys, monkeypatch):
        ws = _make_workspace(tmp_path)

        # boundary share second granularity otherwise (same reason
        # test_feedback_package.py monkeypatches now_iso).
        counter = {"n": 0}
    
        def fake_now():
            counter["n"] += 1

        monkeypatch.setattr(fu, "now_iso", fake_now)
        _record(ws, "run-1")
        _record(ws, "run-2")
        fu.main(["--action", "mark-submitted", "--workspace-root", str(ws)])
        _record(ws, "run-3")  # after the submission boundary
        _write_record(ws, "2026-a.json", "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z")
        _write_record(ws, "2026-b.json", "2026-01-02T00:00:00Z")

    def test_c2_no_project_side_classification_store():
        offenders: list[str] = []
        for path in sorted(MEMORY_DIR.rglob("*")):
            if not path.is_file():
                continue

    def test_c57_shipped_text_adds_no_fresh_neutrality_violation():
        violations: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
        for root_name in ("templates", "shared", "skills", "agents"):
            for path in (FRAMEWORK_ROOT / root_name).rglob("*"):
                if not path.is_file() or path.suffix not in {".md", ".py", ".sh", ".yaml", ".yml", ".json"}:
                    continue
                rel = path.relative_to(FRAMEWORK_ROOT).as_posix()
                text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
                for pattern in (
                    re.compile(r"\b(?:req|requirement)\s+\d{3}\b", re.IGNORECASE),
                    re.compile(r"\bFeature\s+\d{3}\b"),
                    re.compile(r"\.specify/specs/\d{3}-"),
                    re.compile(r"\.specify/features/\d{3}"),
                ):
                    if pattern.search(text):
                        violations.setdefault(rel, []).append(pattern.pattern)
        fresh = sorted(set(violations) - PRE_EXISTING_NEUTRALITY_DEBT)
>       assert not fresh, (

    def record_of(ws: Path, run_id: str) -> dict:
        """Read back one persisted determination record (program-side read)."""
        session = ws / ".specify" / "memory" / "session"
        for path in sorted(session.glob("*.md")):
            match = _FENCE.search(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if not match:
                continue
            record = json.loads(match.group(1))

    def record_of(ws: Path, run_id: str) -> dict:
        """Read back one persisted determination record (program-side read)."""
        session = ws / ".specify" / "memory" / "session"
        for path in sorted(session.glob("*.md")):
            match = _FENCE.search(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if not match:
                continue
            record = json.loads(match.group(1))
            if record.get("run_id") == run_id:
                return record

    def test_chain_runs_with_sockets_blocked(tmp_path, capsys, monkeypatch):
        # FR-014/C-23: zero network for the whole chain — package, ingest and

    def record_of(ws: Path, run_id: str) -> dict:
        """Read back one persisted determination record (program-side read)."""
        session = ws / ".specify" / "memory" / "session"
        for path in sorted(session.glob("*.md")):
            match = _FENCE.search(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            if not match:
                continue
            record = json.loads(match.group(1))
            if record.get("run_id") == run_id:
                return record

    @pytest.mark.contract
    def test_shipped_surface_has_no_framework_runtime_references():
        violations = []

            for path in (REPO_ROOT / root_name).rglob("*"):

[gw96] linux -- Python 3.11.11 /usr/local/bin/python3

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-17596/popen-gw96/test_the_resolution_input_erro0')

    def test_the_resolution_input_error_shapes_refuse_without_inventing_a_spec_dir(tmp_path):
        """The four GW-03 shapes are input errors (exit-2 class), each naming its subject.
    
        Per shape, the whole pipeline runs on its own worktree: the resolution refuses, the reason is
        `closure-input-error`, the line keeps its `completed` state and no merge follows. The branches
        below are the shapes the live registry actually exposes (most lines carry a branch with no
        three-digit prefix), which is why a silent skip would have made the gate a no-op for most of
        the fleet.
        """
        shapes = [
            # (name, branch, features rows, expected subject fragment)
            ("branchless", "feature-no-id", [("081", ".specify/specs/081/requirements.md")], "branch"),
            ("no-row", "081-norow", [("999", ".specify/specs/999/requirements.md")], "081"),
            ("dash-cell", "081-dash", [("081", "-")], "Spec Path"),
            ("missing-dir", "081-missing", [("081", ".specify/specs/999-absent/requirements.md")],
             "999-absent"),
        ]
        reg = tmp_path / "reg"
        reg.mkdir()
        for name, branch, rows, subject in shapes:
            repo, wt = _make_line_repo(tmp_path, name, branch,
                                       spec_cell=".specify/specs/unused/requirements.md",
                                       fixture=None, features_rows=rows)
            line_id = _register_line(reg, repo, wt, branch)
            proc, summary = _run_integrator(tmp_path, repo, reg, line_id)
            assert proc.returncode != 0, "%s: an unresolvable spec dir must refuse the merge" % name
            row = _result_for(summary, line_id)
            assert row["reason"] == "closure-input-error", (
                "%s: the shape is an input error, not a block and not a `quality-gate`: %r"
                % (name, row)
            )
>           assert subject in row.get("detail", ""), (
                "%s: the refusal must name its subject %r; got %r" % (name, subject, row.get("detail"))
            )
E           AssertionError: no-row: the refusal must name its subject '081'; got 'feature registry not found: /tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-17596/popen-gw96/test_the_resolution_input_erro0/wt-no-row/.specify/features.md'
E           assert '081' in 'feature registry not found: /tmp/pytest-of-agent/pytest-17596/popen-gw96/test_the_resolution_input_erro0/wt-no-row/.specify/features.md'

    def test_us1_record_rejects_ungrounded_semantic_finding(tmp_path):
        """A semantic verdict without commit/path evidence is schema-rejected (C-3)."""
        ws = assemble_workspace(tmp_path)
        run_engine(["--action", "collect", "--workspace-root", str(ws)], cwd=ws)
        target = ".specify/todo/20260812-backlog.md"
        finding = {
            "id": hashlib.sha1(f"stale-residue|{target}".encode()).hexdigest()[:12],
            "category": "stale-residue", "target": target, "severity": "high",
            "summary": "judged stale after analysis",
            "evidenceRefs": [],  # no commit/path evidence — agent must not do this
            "detection": "semantic", "disposition": "delete",
            "reversibility": "irreversible", "state": "pending",
        }
        verdicts = ws / "verdicts.json"
        verdicts.write_text(json.dumps({"findings": [finding]}), encoding="utf-8")
        proc = run_engine(
            ["--action", "record", "--file", str(verdicts), "--workspace-root", str(ws)], cwd=ws)
        assert proc.returncode == 2
